2026-10-29T22:17:37.9568916Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Serve the round-3 PP4 bundle with a fresh image

One image build and one PP4 serving run (c232, no [profile]) for the round-3
bundle on top of the 9590.42 configuration (40925558339, serving commit
9813498, image 40911121619). The image must be rebuilt: the DeepGEMM host
series gained sm90-prefill-lean, sm90-prefill-tail-pair and
sm90-1d2d-decode-bm192 (check-firmus-image.sh rejects 40911121619), and
kernels/fa3-verify compiles the two-heads-per-CTA rows. Under
[require-build] provision rewrites the PP4 pins to this run's image and
check-firmus-pp4-cpu.py accepts it through the sidecar naming this commit.

Changes in the bundle and expected effect (power-cap factor in brackets)
Prefill (period 117.2 / 117.1 / 120.3 ms at 9590.42)
1. MegaMoE four-stage-lean (04fd636, DG_SM90_PREFILL_LEAN=1): EP1 counts go
   straight into the polled sums, SM 0 republication and the pull barrier
   are skipped, stmatrix CD stores; bit-exact. -7..-13 us per layer
   [0.36-0.6, sync removal], -0.17..-0.31 ms per 24-layer stage.
2. MegaMoE four-stage-lean-pair (2dc907e, also DG_SM90_PREFILL_TAIL_PAIR=1):
   tail tiles pooled over two N blocks; bit-exact. A further -10..-55 us per
   layer [0.6-1]. The tail-split, direct-FC2 and A-gather variants that lost
   in round 2 are removed.
3. One-pass Prefill MoE input (1292d97, SGLANG_PREFILL_MOE_INPUT=onepass):
   residual add, RMSNorm, FP8 input and scales, gate GEMM and router in one
   Triton kernel without writing the BF16 normalized activation; residual
   bit-equal to fused, FP8 codes within one E4M3 step of the FP64 reference.
   -0.82..-1.10 ms per stage period [1, bytes and a kernel removed].
4. FP32 Q/K norm pinned (7174620; won on every GPU in 40911121619 and
   40925558339) and the QKV/O DeepGEMM schedule removed (lost by 12% in
   both runs): no kernel change, about 25-35 s less Decode-GPU selection time.
Decode (step 34.4 ms at cap 48)
5. Fused Decode TARGET_VERIFY MoE input (bf8ece5, regenerated on top of 3 in
   3e86047; SGLANG_DECODE_MOE_INPUT=fused): the post-attention norm writes
   the FP8 input and the router the top-k with the graph-padding mask; no
   pre-dispatch kernel. -0.19..-0.30 ms per step (-0.55..-0.9%) [1].
6. Two-KV-heads-per-CTA FA3 verify rows (7ba8cf2, p128split2-h2-fp8 and
   p128split3-h2-fp8): 256 B contiguous row reads instead of 128 B; same FP
   and tensor instruction counts as the pin, 168 registers, no spill. Pays
   off only if the 2.7 TB/s FA3 ceiling comes from the read granularity:
   0..-1.7 ms per step [1]. A diagnostic Triton probe reads the same KV at
   128/256/512 B and records TB/s in fa3.json either way.
7. Decode QKV BLOCK_M 192 tile for 129..192 rows (7ba8cf2,
   QKV>128:192,72,5,1,0): 176-192 rows in one M tile instead of two, -25%
   tensor work at the same bytes per CTA. -0.3..-0.55 ms per step [1].
System
8. POST /freeze_gc to every worker after the sanity chat (26e8a39): targets
   the periodic ~0.33 s Decode output stall every 14-19 s (Python GC);
   +0.35% in the serving model, +1.0% if the measured stall share holds.
9. Concurrency 225 -> 232 (26e8a39): +0.33-0.40% in the serving model at the
   current kernels. With Prefill -3% and Decode -5%, c225 and c232 differ by
   0.2% (10,020 against 10,000); c232 is the better choice at the smaller
   Decode gains expected here, so it stays.

Totals: Prefill -1.1..-2.1% (low: 1 + 3; high: 1 + 2 + 3); Decode -1.5..-7.4%
(low: 5 alone; high: 5 + 6 + 7). Serving model (pp4-serving-model.py,
r3 calibration): about 9,700-9,900 at these totals; 10,000 needs Prefill
-4% with Decode -2%, which this bundle reaches only at the high end of 2.

In-job gates (each falls back to the control or the pin)
- Provision: PP4 CPU gate on the new image (29 SGLang overlays applied in
  order to the extracted tree), check-firmus-image.sh (host series recorded
  in the image equals patches/deep-gemm-host/series, now nine patches).
- Prefill GPUs, three workers in parallel: select-firmus-prefill-megamoe.py
  with control, four-stage, four-stage-lean and four-stage-lean-pair on 12
  GPUs x 2 routings plus an untimed "edges" routing for exactness; a
  variant is adopted only if bit-exact everywhere and faster in total.
- Decode GPUs, one after the other while the MegaMoE selection runs:
  (1) select-firmus-prefill-attention.py, onepass against fused (residual
  bit-equal, FP8/top-k against the FP64 gate, faster on all four GPUs);
  (2) select-firmus-decode-kernels.sh fa3 --pinned-env: h2 rows, byte
  equality where the split equals the pin, >= 1 us/layer on every GPU at
  44/46/48 in both blocks; (3) check-firmus-pp4-copy-engine.py;
  (4) select-firmus-decode-kernels.sh moe-input: residual, FP8 input,
  scales and top-k equal to pre-dispatch from the same y, max(1 us, 2%) per
  layer at 44/46/48 in both blocks on every GPU; (5)
  select-firmus-decode-kernels.sh dense --pinned-env: bit-identical to the
  pinned QKV config, >= 0.5 us faster pooled and in both blocks at 176, 184
  and 192 rows; only the changed DG_SM90_1D2D_DECODE_CONFIG line is appended.
- freeze_gc: failures are only logged to freeze-gc.log.

Timeline estimate (40925558339 ended at 11:46)
- Decode-GPU group: onepass 35-45 s, FA3 h2 + probe about 35 s, copy engine
  about 20 s, MoE input 30-45 s, dense 45-55 s -> 165-200 s against about
  145 s before; the MegaMoE group runs three candidates instead of five.
  Workers start 20-55 s later -> Slurm elapsed about 12:05-12:40, more than
  4 minutes inside the 17-minute limit.

Judging checklist
- results/score.json: eligible, score against 9590.42; TTFT failures (122,
  first 10 s) and ITL failures (5) in the AIPerf export.
- results/prefill-megamoe/selection.json: winner, per-variant exactness on
  all twelve GPUs, totals; the env line in prefill-kernels.env.
- results/prefill-attention/selection.json: onepass against fused per GPU.
- results/decode-selection/{fa3,moe-input,dense}.json and the appended
  lines of source/decode-kernels.env; fa3.json read_probe TB/s at 128, 256
  and 512 B.
- freeze-gc.log for every worker; Decode output gaps against the 0.33 s
  stalls every 14-19 s.
- PP0 period per worker against 117.2 / 117.1 / 120.3 ms; Decode
  #running-req per rank, ITL mean / p99 against 15.60 / 18.47 ms; telemetry
  SM clocks.
